# Code map: packages/story-block

Generated by `npm run codemap` from each file's job sentence (docs/codemap/jobs-story-block.json, the package's docs/dev/codemap.md, or the file's opening comment) and the source. Do not edit by hand. The maps carry no line counts (the 450-line limit is enforced by the size check).

| `testkit/matrix/index-parts/data.js` | The matrix data: its cases, the not-expressible list, its rows and its version. | `CASES`<br>`NOT_EXPRESSIBLE`<br>`ROWS`<br>`MATRIX_VERSION` | `testkit/matrix/rows.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/version.js` | `testkit/matrix/index.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/index-parts/loading.js` | Finding and loading the modules, and running the matrix over them. | `discoverRoots`<br>`loadModules`<br>`runMatrix` | `testkit/matrix/modules.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run.js` | `testkit/matrix/index.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/index-parts/modules.js` | The module folders and the order the matrix runs them in. | `FOLDERS`<br>`MODULES`<br>`ORDER` | `testkit/matrix/modules.js` | `testkit/matrix/index.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/index-parts/report.js` | The matrix report: the testkit description, details, table and summary line. | `describeTestkit`<br>`formatDetails`<br>`formatTable`<br>`summaryLine` | `testkit/matrix/report.js` | `testkit/matrix/index.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/index.js` | The combination matrix, vendored with the rest of the testkit. | `CASES`<br>`NOT_EXPRESSIBLE`<br>`ROWS`<br>`MATRIX_VERSION`<br>`FOLDERS`<br>`MODULES`<br>`ORDER`<br>`discoverRoots`<br>`loadModules`<br>`runMatrix`<br>`describeTestkit`<br>`formatDetails`<br>`formatTable`<br>`summaryLine` | `testkit/matrix/index-parts/data.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/index-parts/loading.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/index-parts/modules.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/index-parts/report.js` | `scripts/matrix.mjs` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/modules-parts/discovery.js` | Finds the module checkouts that sit next to each other in a folder, by their usual folder names. | `FOLDERS`<br>`discoverRoots(base)` | — | `testkit/matrix/modules.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/modules-parts/loading.js` | Imports each matrix module's definition builder from its checkout and sorts the missing and broken ones. | `loadModules(roots = {}, entries = {})` | `testkit/matrix/modules-parts/scripts.js` | `testkit/matrix/modules.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/modules-parts/scripts.js` | The scripted reply, action and facts of each combination-matrix module, plus the order they are registered in. | `ORDER`<br>`MODULES` | — | `testkit/matrix/modules-parts/loading.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/modules.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/modules.js` | The module loaders and scripts of the combination matrix (docs/v2/MATRIX.md): each module's real definition is imported from its checkout by path and given a scripted reply, an action and a few facts… | `ORDER`<br>`MODULES`<br>`FOLDERS`<br>`discoverRoots`<br>`loadModules` | `testkit/matrix/modules-parts/discovery.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/modules-parts/loading.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/modules-parts/scripts.js` | `testkit/matrix/index-parts/loading.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/index-parts/modules.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/basics.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/features.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/registration.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/checks-parts/dormancy.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/checks-parts/registered.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host-parts/boot.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host-parts/keys.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host-parts/peers.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host-parts/script.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/report.js` | the matrix report: the rows-by-cases Markdown table, details, summary line and testkit description. | `formatTable(results)`<br>`formatDetails(results, { verbose = false } = {})`<br>`summaryLine({ counts, ok })`<br>`describeTestkit()` | `testkit/matrix/rows.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/version.js` | `testkit/matrix/index-parts/report.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/rows.js` | the supported Story Suite combinations and the matrix case columns in report order. | `ROWS`<br>`CASES`<br>`NOT_EXPRESSIBLE` | — | `testkit/matrix/index-parts/data.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/report.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run.js` | The matrix runner (docs/v2/MATRIX.md): for each combination row it boots the real module definitions in createHost, registers them once, plays a scripted reply, runs each module's action and replays… | `runMatrix({ loaded = {}, missing = {}, broken = {}, rows = null, only = null, cases = null, strict = false, onResult = null } = {})` | `testkit/matrix/rows.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/basics.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/checks.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/revisions.js` | `testkit/matrix/index-parts/loading.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run/basics.js` | the matrix basic row, action, replay, prompt and dormancy cases. | `basics` | `testkit/matrix/modules.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/checks.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host.js` | `testkit/matrix/run.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/features.js` | Matrix cases for module features and the story clock: feature off and unset clock. | `featureOff`<br>`unsetClock` | `testkit/matrix/modules.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/shared-na.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/checks.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host.js` | `testkit/matrix/run/cases.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/peers.js` | Matrix cases for peer modules that are absent from a row: the missing query and the malformed answer. | `missingQuery`<br>`malformed` | `testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/shared-na.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/checks.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host.js` | `testkit/matrix/run/cases.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/registration.js` | Matrix cases for module registration: SD disabled, late registration and duplicate name. | `disabledSd`<br>`lateRegistration`<br>`duplicateName` | `testkit/matrix/modules.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/shared-na.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/checks.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host.js` | `testkit/matrix/run/cases.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/shared-na.js` | The "not applicable" result a matrix case returns when its row has nothing to check. | `na` | — | `testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/features.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/peers.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/registration.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run/cases.js` | the matrix missing-query, malformed, feature, clock, disabled, late and duplicate cases. | `missingQuery`<br>`malformed`<br>`featureOff`<br>`unsetClock`<br>`disabledSd`<br>`lateRegistration`<br>`duplicateName` | `testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/features.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/peers.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/cases-parts/registration.js` | `testkit/matrix/run.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run/checks-parts/dormancy.js` | Dormancy probe for the matrix runner and the gap notes it reports. | `gap`<br>`dormancy`<br>`gapNote` | `testkit/matrix/modules.js` | `testkit/matrix/run/checks.js` | — |
| `testkit/matrix/run/checks-parts/registered.js` | Registration and held-back-message probes of a matrix host. | `checkRegistered`<br>`heldBack` | `testkit/matrix/modules.js`<br>`testkit/matrix/run/host.js` | `testkit/matrix/run/checks.js` | — |

## Feature entry points

| Feature | Start reading at | What it covers |
|---|---|---|
| Extension entry | `index.js` | What SillyTavern loads: wires the v2 runtime, packs and diagnostics. |
| Lean RPG (rolls, fights, gear) | `src/v2/lean-rpg/index.js` | The optional RPG module: checks, the fight board, gear and levels. |
| Fight board | `src/v2/lean-rpg/board/commit.js` | Commits staged board choices as the reply's one guarded RPG resolution. |
| Data modules and block editor | `src/v2/data-modules/block-builder.js` | Builds and edits data-module definitions in the in-chat editor. |

## One job per file: backlog

Files with more than 4 exports (legacy; pure re-export facades are exempt; split when moving out of `packages/`).

- `src/v2/domains/scene/constants-parts/you.js`: 5 exports
- `testkit/fake-st.js`: 9 exports
